~~~text
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/implementation/T037.md:3:用户在核对改动方案与文件范围后明确说「开始吧」，授权按本方案修复 T037。范围与验收沿用 FR-008 和 SC-004。此前隔离复现覆盖直接命令、Code Mode 及单行对照。 [原任务](../tasks.md#p0-提交停写回归) · [复现证据](../analysis/recording-acceptance.md#t037-隔离复现)
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/implementation/T037.md:5:1. 在 `packages/skills/tests/test_conversation_recording.py` 增加真实安装后的回调测试。分别覆盖多行只读核对后停止写入，以及动态 Code Mode 调用在宿主命令到齐前暂缓，失败及成功的提交过程均不被后续回调补写。先在旧实现运行并核对失败点。
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/implementation/T037.md:6:2. 在 `recording/events.py` 用实际 `CommandExecution.command` 参数数组识别命令。shell 的 `-c` 参数按完整脚本解析，直接工具输入沿用原判断。按当轮宿主事件给 Code Mode 调用判定是否可落盘，提交调用及其结果不进入详情；普通修复恢复记录。`scripts/post_tool_use.py` 使用相同判定约束回调补写，`Stop` 继续消费共同的停写结果。
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/implementation/T037.md:9:审查后补充实际宿主事件的边界。多行引号、续行和重定向正文按 shell 的词边界处理。Code Mode 返回仍在运行的 cell 时，后续 `wait` 继续沿用该调用的记录判定，直到整个调用有终态。失败提交后的 `FileChange` 和包含正常修复的混合命令恢复记录。对应安装回调测试均先复现失败，再随修复复验。 [验收记录](../analysis/recording-acceptance.md#t037-正式本地安装复验)
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/subagent-recording-timeline.md:25:| 派发输入原文         | V1 和 V2 的父会话 `PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse` 均在 `tool_input.message` 提供原文，与两条含空行的合成输入逐字一致。                                                                                                                                                |
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/subagent-recording-timeline.md:33:本轮确认了直接派发时的父会话输入来源，以及父会话空闲和活跃时的子代理回复回调。正式主记录的交错写入和分隔仍待接入并复验 SC-010、SC-011。后续补充消息和 Code Mode 中的派发未在本轮实测。 [验收标准](../spec.md#measurable-outcomes) · [早期子代理取证](content-source.md#子代理活动及父会话可见字段)
~~~

~~~text
${String.raw`"""沿原生 Code Mode 验证合成子代理的派发、补充及回调到达时的日志。"""

import hashlib
import http.server
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import runpy
import shlex
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid

WORK = Path(sys.argv[2])
PROMPTS = {
    "first": "FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_INITIAL",
    "second": "FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_SUPPLEMENT",
    "third": "FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_CROSS_TURN",
}
PARENT_FINAL = {
    "first": "FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_FIRST_DONE\n\n父代理首轮已自行回复。",
    "second": "FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_SECOND_DONE\n\n父代理补充派发后已自行回复。",
    "third": "FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_THIRD_DONE\n\n父代理继续第三轮。",
}
INPUTS = {
    "initial": "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_INITIAL\n\n子代理初次派发的合成原文。",
    "supplement": "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_SUPPLEMENT\n\n对同一子代理的合成补充。",
}
ANSWERS = {
    "initial": "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_INITIAL_DONE\n\n初次回复原文。",
    "supplement": "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_SUPPLEMENT_DONE\n\n补充回复原文。",
}
DISCOVERY_CODE = '''
const catalog = typeof ALL_TOOLS === "undefined" ? Object.keys(tools) : ALL_TOOLS.map((tool) => tool.name);
text("FOUNDRY_CATALOG " + JSON.stringify(catalog.filter((name) => /spawn_agent|followup_task|send_input/.test(name))));
'''
NATIVE_HOOKS = ("UserPromptSubmit", "PreToolUse", "PostToolUse", "SubagentStart", "SubagentStop", "Stop")


def read_reply_in_callback(payload):
    """只读取本次合成运行中回调指向、且身份匹配的文件。"""
    path = Path(payload["agent_transcript_path"]).resolve()
    assert path.is_relative_to(WORK / "state/sessions") and path.stem.endswith(payload["agent_id"])
    active_turn = None
    finals = []
    completed = False
    offset = 0
    with path.open("rb") as log:
        for line_number, line in enumerate(log, 1):
            if not line.endswith(b"\n"):
                break
            event = json.loads(line)
            body = event.get("payload", {})
            kind = body.get("type")
            if kind == "session_meta":
                assert body["id"] == payload["agent_id"]
            if event.get("type") == "session_meta":
                assert body["id"] == payload["agent_id"]
            if kind == "task_started":
                active_turn = body.get("turn_id")
            if active_turn == payload["turn_id"]:
                if kind == "task_complete" and body.get("turn_id") == active_turn:
                    completed = True
                if event["type"] == "response_item" and kind == "message" and body.get("role") == "assistant" \
                        and body.get("phase") == "final_answer":
                    text = "".join(part["text"] for part in body.get("content", [])
                                   if part.get("type") == "output_text")
                    if text == payload["last_assistant_message"]:
                        finals.append({"time": event["timestamp"], "line": line_number, "byteOffset": offset,
                                       "sha256": hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()})
            offset += len(line)
    return {"activeTurn": active_turn, "matchingFinalAnswers": finals, "taskCompleteAlreadyReadable": completed,
            "completeBytesRead": offset}


if sys.argv[1] == "--observe-hook":
    received = time.time_ns()
    event = sys.argv[3]
    payload = json.load(sys.stdin)
    reply = read_reply_in_callback(payload) if event == "SubagentStop" and payload.get("agent_transcript_path") else None
    row = {"kind": "Hook", "configuredEvent": event, "wallNs": received,
           "payload": payload, "replyAtCallback": reply}
    with (WORK / "observations.jsonl").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        stream.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
    print("{}")
    raise SystemExit


FIXTURE = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).with_name("probe-library.py")))
BINARY = FIXTURE["BINARY"]
SOURCE = FIXTURE["SOURCE"]
MODE = FIXTURE["MODE"]
repo = FIXTURE["repo"]
state = FIXTURE["state"]
LOG = FIXTURE["LOG"]
Endpoint = FIXTURE["Endpoint"]
append = FIXTURE["append"]
git, start, end, send, screen, tmux, wait, hooks = (FIXTURE[x] for x in
    ("git", "start", "end", "send", "screen", "tmux", "wait", "hooks"))
read_back, rollout_row, checkpoint, gate_release, json_lines = (FIXTURE[x] for x in
    ("read_back", "rollout_row", "checkpoint", "gate_release", "json_lines"))
FIXTURE["PARENT_PROMPT"].update(PROMPTS)
FIXTURE["PARENT_REPLY"].update(PARENT_FINAL)


def visible_result(output):
    return output if isinstance(output, str) else json.dumps(output, ensure_ascii=False)


def marker(output, name):
    body = visible_result(output)
    match = re.search(re.escape(name) + r" (.+)", body)
    assert match, (name, body[:1800])
    return json.JSONDecoder().raw_decode(match.group(1))[0]


def locator(value):
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for name in ("task_name", "agent_id"):
            if isinstance(value.get(name), str):
                return value[name]
        for nested in value.values():
            if result := locator(nested):
                return result
    if isinstance(value, list):
        for part in value:
            if result := locator(part):
                return result
    if isinstance(value, str):
        found = re.search(r'"(?:task_name|agent_id)"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"', value)
        if found:
            return found.group(1)
    return None


def choose(catalog, name):
    matches = [candidate for candidate in catalog if candidate == name or
               candidate.endswith(f"__{name}") or candidate.endswith(f".{name}")]
    assert len(matches) == 1, (name, catalog)
    return matches[0]


def nested_code(tool, stage, target=None):
    lines = json.dumps(INPUTS[stage].split("\n"), ensure_ascii=False)
    if stage == "initial":
        argument = ({"task_name": "timeline_code", "fork_turns": "none"} if MODE.startswith("v2")
                    else {"fork_context": False})
    else:
        argument = {"target": target}
    return (f'const lines = {lines};\n'
            f'const result = await tools[{json.dumps(tool)}]({{...{json.dumps(argument)}, message: lines.join("\\n")}});\n'
            'text("FOUNDRY_NESTED_RESULT " + JSON.stringify(result));')


class CodeEndpoint(Endpoint):
    def run_request(self):
        request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
        history = request["input"]
        output = {row["call_id"]: row.get("output") for row in history if row.get("type") in
                  ("function_call_output", "custom_tool_call_output")}
        user_texts = [part.get("text", "") for row in history if row.get("role") == "user"
                      for part in row.get("content", []) if isinstance(part, dict)]
        child_texts = user_texts + [part.get("encrypted_content", part.get("text", ""))
                                   for row in history if row.get("type") == "agent_message"
                                   for part in row.get("content", []) if isinstance(part, dict)]
        child = next((label for label in ("supplement", "initial")
                      if any(INPUTS[label] in item for item in child_texts)), None)
        parent = next((label for label in ("third", "second", "first")
                       if any(PROMPTS[label] in item for item in user_texts)), None)
        auxiliary = "text" in request or not request.get("tools")
        append("Request", correlation=child or parent or "other", auxiliary=auxiliary,
               exactChildInput=(INPUTS[child] in child_texts if child else None), output=output)
        if child and not auxiliary:
            assert self.server.gates[child].wait(50), f"未放行 {child}"
            item = self.final(ANSWERS[child])
        elif parent and not auxiliary:
            item = self.parent_item(parent, request["tools"], output)
        elif auxiliary:
            item = self.final("FOUNDRY_CODE_AUXILIARY")
        else:
            append("UnexpectedRequest", userTexts=user_texts, contentTypes=[e.get("type") for e in history])
            item = self.final("FOUNDRY_CODE_UNEXPECTED")
        self.emit(item)
        append("Response", correlation=child or parent or "other", auxiliary=auxiliary, type=item["type"])

    def parent_item(self, parent, tools, output):
        if "code-discover" not in output and parent == "first":
            matches = [(None, entry) for entry in tools if entry.get("name") == "exec"]
            matches.extend((entry["name"], sub) for entry in tools for sub in entry.get("tools", [])
                           if sub.get("name") == "exec")
            assert len(matches) == 1, [(tool.get("type"), tool.get("name")) for tool in tools]
            namespace, entry = matches[0]
            append("CodeDefinition", namespace=namespace, name=entry["name"], type=entry.get("type"))
            self.server.namespace = namespace
            return self.call("code-discover", DISCOVERY_CODE)
        if parent == "first" and "code-spawn" not in output:
            catalog = marker(output["code-discover"], "FOUNDRY_CATALOG")
            append("ToolCatalog", names=catalog)
            self.server.spawn = choose(catalog, "spawn_agent")
            self.server.follow = choose(catalog, "followup_task" if MODE.startswith("v2") else "send_input")
            return self.call("code-spawn", nested_code(self.server.spawn, "initial"))
        if parent == "first" or parent == "second":
            if "code-spawn" in output:
                result = marker(output["code-spawn"], "FOUNDRY_NESTED_RESULT")
                target = locator(result)
                assert target, result
                self.server.target = target
            assert self.server.target
        if parent == "second" and "code-followup" not in output:
            return self.call("code-followup", nested_code(self.server.follow, "supplement", self.server.target))
        if parent == "second":
            result = marker(output["code-followup"], "FOUNDRY_NESTED_RESULT")
            append("FollowupToolReturned", result=result)
        if parent == "third":
            assert self.server.gates["parent-third"].wait(30), "未放行第三轮父代理回复"
        return self.final(PARENT_FINAL[parent])

    def call(self, call_id, code):
        item = {"type": "custom_tool_call", "id": f"ct-{call_id}", "call_id": call_id,
                "name": "exec", "input": code}
        if self.server.namespace:
            item["namespace"] = self.server.namespace
        append("IssuedCode", id=call_id, code=code)
        return item


def hook_events(event, message=None):
    rows = [item for item in json_lines(LOG) if item.get("configuredEvent") == event]
    if message is None:
        return rows
    return [item for item in rows if message == item["payload"].get("last_assistant_message")]


def configure():
    repo.mkdir()
    git("init", "-q")
    (repo / "README.md").write_text("Code Mode 合成验证\n", encoding="utf-8")
    git("add", "README.md")
    git("-c", "user.name=Timeline Fixture", "-c", "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
        "commit", "-qm", "synthetic fixture")
    maintained = SOURCE / "packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording"
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(maintained / "install.py"), "--project", str(repo)],
                   cwd=repo, check=True, capture_output=True)
    installed = repo / ".agents/skills/conversation-recording"
    files = {path.relative_to(maintained).as_posix(): hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
             for path in maintained.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
    assert len(files) == 17
    assert all(hashlib.sha256((installed / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest
               for name, digest in files.items())
    append("Install", version=subprocess.check_output([BINARY, "--version"], text=True).strip(),
           mode=MODE, hashes=files)
    path = repo / ".codex/hooks.json"
    config = json.loads(path.read_text())
    assert set(config["hooks"]) == {"UserPromptSubmit", "PostToolUse", "Stop"}
    for event in NATIVE_HOOKS:
        command = shlex.join([sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--observe-hook", str(WORK), event])
        config["hooks"].setdefault(event, []).append({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command}]})
    path.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def trust_project(path):
    assert not hooks()
    start()
    wait(lambda: "Trust and continue" in screen(), "项目目录首次信任")
    time.sleep(0.33)
    tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")
    wait(lambda: "Trust all and continue" in screen(), "Hook 首次信任")
    original = hooks()
    assert original and all(entry["trustStatus"] == "untrusted" for entry in original)
    tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Down")
    wait(lambda: "› 2. Trust all" in screen(), "选中确认 Hook")
    tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")
    wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "临时终端就绪")
    trusted = hooks()
    assert len(trusted) == len(original)
    assert all(entry["trustStatus"] == "trusted" and entry["enabled"]
               and Path(entry["sourcePath"]) == path for entry in trusted)
    append("NativeTrust", hooks=[entry["eventName"] for entry in trusted])
    end()
    assert not list(repo.rglob("records/*/*.md"))


def seen_request(label):
    return any(row["kind"] == "Request" and row["correlation"] == label and not row["auxiliary"]
               for row in json_lines(LOG))


def child_finished(stage):
    row = wait(lambda: rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", ANSWERS[stage]),
               f"{stage} 子线程完成")
    stop = wait(lambda: hook_events("SubagentStop", ANSWERS[stage]), f"{stage} 停止回调")[-1]
    assert stop["payload"]["turn_id"] == row["payload"]["turn_id"]
    assert Path(stop["payload"]["agent_transcript_path"]).name == row["file"]
    append("ChildCompleteReadBack", label=stage, **row)
    return stop


def run():
    path = configure()
    with http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), CodeEndpoint) as server:
        threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        server.gates = {name: threading.Event() for name in ("initial", "supplement", "parent-third")}
        server.target = None
        code_mode_flags = (
            f'multi_agent_v2 = {str(MODE.startswith("v2")).lower()}\n'
            f'multi_agent_v2_dynamic_tools = {str(MODE == "v2-dynamic").lower()}'
        )
        FIXTURE["config_path"].write_text(f'''model = "gpt-5.4"
model_provider = "fixture"
check_for_update_on_startup = false
approval_policy = "never"
sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"
[features]
code_mode = true
{code_mode_flags}
[model_providers.fixture]
name = "Isolated Code Mode"
base_url = "http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/v1"
wire_api = "responses"
requires_openai_auth = false
request_max_retries = 0
stream_max_retries = 0
''', encoding="utf-8")
        try:
            trust_project(path)
            start()
            wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "普通终端启动")
            send(PROMPTS["first"])
            wait(lambda: seen_request("initial"), "子代理收到初始合成派发", timeout=22)
            parent = read_back("first")
            wait(lambda: rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", PARENT_FINAL["first"], parent.stem),
                 "父首轮实际结束")
            assert not hook_events("SubagentStop", ANSWERS["initial"])
            checkpoint("parent-first-done")
            gate_release(server, "initial")
            first_stop = child_finished("initial")
            checkpoint("child-initial-done")

            send(PROMPTS["second"])
            wait(lambda: seen_request("supplement"), "同一子线程收到合成补充", timeout=22)
            assert read_back("second") == parent
            wait(lambda: rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", PARENT_FINAL["second"], parent.stem),
                 "父第二轮实际结束")
            assert not hook_events("SubagentStop", ANSWERS["supplement"])
            checkpoint("parent-second-done")

            send(PROMPTS["third"])
            wait(lambda: seen_request("third"), "第三轮已经抵达父模型")
            assert rollout_row("user_message", "message", PROMPTS["third"], parent.stem)
            checkpoint("parent-third-started")
            gate_release(server, "supplement")
            second_stop = child_finished("supplement")
            checkpoint("child-supplement-cross-turn-done")
            assert second_stop["payload"]["agent_id"] == first_stop["payload"]["agent_id"]
            assert second_stop["payload"]["turn_id"] != first_stop["payload"]["turn_id"]
            gate_release(server, "parent-third")
            assert read_back("third") == parent
            wait(lambda: rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", PARENT_FINAL["third"], parent.stem),
                 "父第三轮实际结束")
            end()
            verify(parent)
        finally:
            (WORK / "last-screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")
            for gate in server.gates.values():
                gate.set()
            try:
                tmux("kill-server")
            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
                pass
            server.shutdown()


def verify(parent):
    entries = json_lines(LOG)
    assert [(entry["id"]) for entry in entries if entry["kind"] == "IssuedCode"] == [
        "code-discover", "code-spawn", "code-followup"]
    assert not [entry for entry in entries if entry["kind"] in ("RequestError", "UnexpectedRequest")]
    for stage in INPUTS:
        child = [entry for entry in entries if entry["kind"] == "Request"
                 and entry["correlation"] == stage and not entry["auxiliary"]]
        assert child and all(entry["exactChildInput"] for entry in child), child
    append("RunCompleted", sessionId=parent.stem, record=parent.relative_to(repo).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({"mode": MODE, "sessionId": parent.stem, "record": parent.relative_to(repo).as_posix()}))


run()
`}
~~~

~~~text
const checks=await Promise.allSettled(["v2","v1"].map(mode=>tools.exec_command({
  cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - ${load("code_probe_dir_"+mode)}/observations.jsonl <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json, sys
path = Path(sys.argv[1])
entries = [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()] if path.exists() else []
for row in entries[-19:]:
    if row["kind"] in ("ToolCatalog", "CodeDefinition", "RequestError", "FollowupToolReturned"):
        print({key: value for key, value in row.items() if key not in ("monotonicNs", "wallNs")})
    else:
        print(row["kind"], row.get("correlation") or row.get("configuredEvent") or row.get("label") or row.get("id") or "")
PY`,max_output_tokens:1600
})));
checks.forEach((r,i)=>{if(r.status==="fulfilled")text({mode:["v2","v1"][i],...r.value});else text({mode:["v2","v1"][i],error:String(r.reason)});});
~~~

~~~text
const repo="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init",work=load("timeline_work");
const template=load("code_probe_cmd_template").replace("/usr/bin/timeout 110","/usr/bin/timeout 155");
store("code_probe_cmd_template",template);
const results=await Promise.allSettled(["v2","v1"].map(mode=>{const dir=`${work}/cm${mode.slice(1)}`;
  store("code_probe_dir_"+mode,dir);
  return tools.exec_command({cmd:template.replaceAll("CODEPROBE_DIR",dir).replaceAll("CODEPROBE_MODE",mode),workdir:repo,yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:3250});
}));
results.forEach((r,i)=>{const mode=["v2","v1"][i];if(r.status==="fulfilled"){text({mode,...r.value});if(r.value.session_id)store("code_probe_session_"+mode,r.value.session_id);}else text({mode,error:String(r.reason)});});

~~~

~~~text
from pathlib import Path
import json, sys
path = Path(sys.argv[1])
entries = [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()] if path.exists() else []
for row in entries[-25:]:
    if row["kind"] in ("ToolCatalog", "CodeDefinition", "RequestError", "FollowupToolReturned"):
~~~

~~~text
            assert rollout_row("task_started", "turn_id", third["payload"]["turn_id"], parent.stem)'''
assert text.count(old)==1
path.write_text(text.replace(old,new))
~~~

~~~text
const scenarios=["v1","v2-dynamic"];
const states=await Promise.allSettled(scenarios.map(mode=>tools.exec_command({
  cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - ${load("code_probe_dir_"+mode)}/observations.jsonl <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json, sys
path = Path(sys.argv[1])
entries = [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()] if path.exists() else []
for row in entries[-23:]:
    if row["kind"] in ("ToolCatalog", "CodeDefinition", "RequestError", "FollowupToolReturned"):
        print({key: value for key, value in row.items() if key not in ("monotonicNs", "wallNs")})
    elif row["kind"] == "Hook" and row["configuredEvent"] == "SubagentStop":
        print("SubagentStop", row["replyAtCallback"])
    else:
        print(row["kind"], row.get("correlation") or row.get("configuredEvent") or row.get("label") or row.get("id") or "")
PY`,max_output_tokens:2000
})));
states.forEach((r,i)=>{if(r.status==="fulfilled")text({mode:scenarios[i],...r.value});else text({mode:scenarios[i],error:String(r.reason)});});

~~~

~~~text
from datetime import UTC,datetime
from pathlib import Path
import json,sys
root=Path(sys.argv[1])
entries=list(map(json.loads,(root/"observations.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()))
rid=next(row["sessionId"] for row in entries if row["kind"]=="RunCompleted")
for row in entries:
    event=row.get("configuredEvent")
    if event in ("PreToolUse","PostToolUse","UserPromptSubmit","SubagentStop"):
        p=row["payload"]
        if event in ("PreToolUse","PostToolUse"):
            data=p.get("tool_input")
            data=(data.get("message") if isinstance(data,dict) else str(data)[:36])
            print(event,p.get("tool_name"),p.get("tool_use_id"),data, (str(p.get("tool_response",""))[:300] if event=="PostToolUse" else ""))
        elif p.get("agent_id") or event=="SubagentStop":
            print(event,p["session_id"],p["turn_id"],p.get("agent_id"),p.get("prompt"),row.get("replyAtCallback"))
    if row["kind"] in ("ParentReadBack","GateRelease","ChildCompleteReadBack"):
        stamp=datetime.fromtimestamp(row["wallNs"]/1e9,UTC).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds")
        print(row["kind"],row.get("label") or row.get("turn"),stamp,row.get("timestamp",""))
path=next((root/"state/sessions").rglob(f"*{rid}.jsonl"))
for index,line in enumerate(path.read_text().splitlines(),1):
    row=json.loads(line); p=row["payload"];item=p.get("item") or {}
    if item.get("type")=="CollabAgentToolCall":
        print(index,row["timestamp"],p["turn_id"],item["id"],item["tool"],item["receiver_thread_ids"],item.get("prompt"))
PY`,max_output_tokens:4300});text(r.output);
~~~

~~~text
text=path.read_text()
old='''        if parent == "first" and "code-spawn" not in output:

new='''        if MODE == "v2-direct-control" and parent in ("first", "second"):
            return self.direct_v2_item(parent, tools, output)
        if parent == "first" and "code-spawn" not in output:

new='''    def direct_v2_item(self, parent, tools, output):
        catalog = marker(output["code-discover"], "FOUNDRY_CATALOG")
        assert not catalog, catalog
        if "v2-spawn" not in output:
            namespace, entry = FIXTURE["find_tool"](tools, "spawn_agent")
            append("DirectTool", name=entry["name"], namespace=namespace,
                   nestedCatalog=catalog)
            return FIXTURE["call_item"]("v2-spawn", entry["name"], {
                "task_name": "timeline_code", "fork_turns": "none", "message": INPUTS["initial"]}, namespace)
        if parent == "first":
            self.server.target = locator(output["v2-spawn"])
            assert self.server.target, output["v2-spawn"]
        if parent == "second" and "v2-followup" not in output:
            namespace, entry = FIXTURE["find_tool"](tools, "followup_task")
            append("DirectTool", name=entry["name"], namespace=namespace)
            return FIXTURE["call_item"]("v2-followup", entry["name"], {
                "target": self.server.target, "message": INPUTS["supplement"]}, namespace)
        return self.final(PARENT_FINAL[parent])

    def call(self, call_id, code):'''
assert text.count(old)==1
text=text.replace(old,new)
old='''    assert [(entry["id"]) for entry in entries if entry["kind"] == "IssuedCode"] == [
        "code-discover", "code-spawn", "code-followup"]'''
new='''    expected = ["code-discover"] if MODE == "v2-direct-control" else [
        "code-discover", "code-spawn", "code-followup"]
    assert [entry["id"] for entry in entries if entry["kind"] == "IssuedCode"] == expected
    if MODE == "v2-direct-control":
        assert [entry["name"] for entry in entries if entry["kind"] == "DirectTool"] == [
            "spawn_agent", "followup_task"]'''
assert text.count(old)==1
path.write_text(text.replace(old,new))
~~~

~~~text
{
  "command": "[已省略环境变量值] -lc 'cat > /tmp/foundry-child-timeline.KpE4HAiB/extract-code-mode.py <<'\"'\"'PY'\"'\"'\n\"\"\"独立核对 Code Mode 的合成原文、身份、时间和落盘顺序。\"\"\"\n\nfrom datetime import UTC, datetime\nfrom hashlib import sha256\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\n\nROOT = Path(sys.argv[1])\nMESSAGES = (\n    (\"initial\", \"FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_INITIAL\\n\\n子代理初次派发的合成原文。\",\n     \"FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_INITIAL_DONE\\n\\n初次回复原文。\"),\n    (\"supplement\", \"FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_SUPPLEMENT\\n\\n对同一子代理的合成补充。\",\n     \"FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_SUPPLEMENT_DONE\\n\\n补充回复原文。\"),\n)\nTHIRD = \"FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_CROSS_TURN\"\n\n\ndef read_rows(path):\n    return list(map(json.loads, path.read_text().splitlines()))\n\n\ndef one(rows):\n    matches = list(rows)\n    assert len(matches) == 1, matches\n    return matches[0]\n\n\ndef select(rows, kind, **fields):\n    return one(row for row in rows if row[\"kind\"] == kind\n               and all(row.get(name) == value for name, value in fields.items()))\n\n\ndef stamp(row):\n    return datetime.fromtimestamp(row[\"wallNs\"] / 1_000_000_000, UTC).isoformat(\n        timespec=\"milliseconds\").replace(\"+00:00\", \"Z\")\n\n\ndef match_hook(rows, event, **fields):\n    return one(row for row in rows if row.get(\"configuredEvent\") == event\n               and all(row[\"payload\"].get(name) == value for name, value in fields.items()))\n\n\ndef in_child_call(rows, event, text):\n    return one(row for row in rows if row.get(\"configuredEvent\") == event\n               and isinstance(row[\"payload\"].get(\"tool_input\"), dict)\n               and row[\"payload\"][\"tool_input\"].get(\"message\") == text)\n\n\ndef catalog_check(name, flag):\n    rows = read_rows(ROOT / name / \"observations.jsonl\")\n    tool = select(rows, \"CodeDefinition\")\n    catalog = select(rows, \"ToolCatalog\")\n    request = one(row for row in rows if row[\"kind\"] == \"Request\"\n                  and \"code-discover\" in row.get(\"output\", {}))\n    reply = request[\"output\"][\"code-discover\"]\n    assert tool[\"name\"] == \"exec\" and tool[\"type\"] == \"custom\"\n    assert catalog[\"names\"] == []\n    assert any(part.get(\"text\") == \"FOUNDRY_CATALOG []\" for part in reply)\n    assert not any(row[\"kind\"] == \"RunCompleted\" for row in rows)\n    return {\"dynamicToolsFlag\": flag, \"codeModeCatalogReply\": reply,\n            \"requestedNestedOperations\": [\"spawn_agent\", \"followup_task\", \"send_input\"],\n            \"nestedAgentsMatched\": []}\n\n\ndef check(name, route):\n    work = ROOT / name\n    rows = read_rows(work / \"observations.jsonl\")\n    installation = select(rows, \"Install\")\n    done = select(rows, \"RunCompleted\")\n    parent_id = done[\"sessionId\"]\n    parent_file = one((work / \"state/sessions\").rglob(f\"*{parent_id}.jsonl\"))\n    parent = read_rows(parent_file)\n    record = work / \"repo\" / done[\"record\"]\n    primary = record.read_bytes()\n    assert primary.count(b\"<!-- turn-\") == 3\n    if route == \"nested\":\n        catalog = select(rows, \"ToolCatalog\")\n        assert set(catalog[\"names\"]) == {\"multi_agent_v1__spawn_agent\", \"multi_agent_v1__send_input\"}\n        code_calls = [row[\"payload\"][\"call_id\"] for row in parent if row[\"payload\"].get(\"type\") == \"custom_tool_call\"]\n        assert code_calls == [\"code-discover\", \"code-spawn\", \"code-followup\"]\n    else:\n        tools = [row[\"name\"] for row in rows if row[\"kind\"] == \"DirectTool\"]\n        assert tools == [\"spawn_agent\", \"followup_task\"]\n        assert [row[\"payload\"][\"name\"] for row in parent if row[\"payload\"].get(\"type\") == \"function_call\"] == tools\n    reads = {part: select(rows, \"ParentReadBack\", turn=part) for part in (\"first\", \"second\", \"third\")}\n    assert all(row[\"sessionId\"] == parent_id for row in reads.values())\n    assert sha256(primary).hexdigest() == reads[\"third\"][\"sha256\"]\n    third = match_hook(rows, \"UserPromptSubmit\", prompt=THIRD)\n    parent_third = one(row for row in parent if row[\"payload\"].get(\"type\") == \"task_started\"\n                       and row[\"payload\"][\"turn_id\"] == third[\"payload\"][\"turn_id\"])\n    assert sha256(primary).hexdigest() != reads[\"second\"][\"sha256\"]\n    snapshots = ((select(rows, \"Checkpoint\", label=\"parent-first-done\"),\n                  select(rows, \"Checkpoint\", label=\"child-initial-done\")),\n                 (select(rows, \"Checkpoint\", label=\"parent-third-started\"),\n                  select(rows, \"Checkpoint\", label=\"child-supplement-cross-turn-done\")))\n    assert all(before[\"snapshot\"] == after[\"snapshot\"] for before, after in snapshots)\n\n    events = []\n    for (role, text, reply), parent_label in zip(MESSAGES, (\"first\", \"second\"), strict=True):\n        pre = in_child_call(rows, \"PreToolUse\", text)\n        post = in_child_call(rows, \"PostToolUse\", text)\n        assert pre[\"payload\"][\"tool_use_id\"] == post[\"payload\"][\"tool_use_id\"]\n        tool = post[\"payload\"]\n        called_id = tool[\"tool_use_id\"]\n        stop = match_hook(rows, \"SubagentStop\", last_assistant_message=reply)\n        info = stop[\"payload\"]\n        assert pre[\"payload\"][\"turn_id\"] == tool[\"turn_id\"]\n        assert tool[\"session_id\"] == info[\"session_id\"] == parent_id\n        assert info[\"turn_id\"] != tool[\"turn_id\"]\n        assert text.encode() not in primary and reply.encode() not in primary\n        call = one((index, row) for index, row in enumerate(parent, 1)\n                   if row[\"payload\"].get(\"type\") == \"item_completed\"\n                   and row[\"payload\"].get(\"item\", {}).get(\"id\") == called_id)\n        assert call[1][\"payload\"][\"turn_id\"] == tool[\"turn_id\"]\n        activity = call[1][\"payload\"][\"item\"]\n        if route == \"nested\":\n            assert activity.get(\"type\") == \"CollabAgentToolCall\"\n            assert activity[\"prompt\"] == text\n            assert activity[\"receiver_thread_ids\"] == [info[\"agent_id\"]]\n            response = json.loads(tool[\"tool_response\"])\n            if role == \"initial\":\n                assert response[\"agent_id\"] == info[\"agent_id\"]\n            else:\n                assert response[\"submission_id\"] == info[\"turn_id\"]\n            child_prompt = match_hook(rows, \"UserPromptSubmit\", agent_id=info[\"agent_id\"], prompt=text)\n            assert child_prompt[\"payload\"][\"turn_id\"] == info[\"turn_id\"]\n        else:\n            assert activity.get(\"type\") == \"SubAgentActivity\"\n            assert activity[\"agent_thread_id\"] == info[\"agent_id\"]\n\n        sent = select(rows, \"GateRelease\", label=role)\n        assert reads[parent_label][\"wallNs\"] < sent[\"wallNs\"] < stop[\"wallNs\"]\n        if role == \"supplement\":\n            assert reads[\"second\"][\"wallNs\"] < third[\"wallNs\"] < sent[\"wallNs\"] < stop[\"wallNs\"] < reads[\"third\"][\"wallNs\"]\n            assert parent_third[\"timestamp\"] < stamp(sent)\n        callback = stop[\"replyAtCallback\"]\n        assert callback[\"activeTurn\"] == info[\"turn_id\"]\n        final = one(callback[\"matchingFinalAnswers\"])\n        assert final[\"sha256\"] == sha256(reply.encode()).hexdigest()\n        assert not callback[\"taskCompleteAlreadyReadable\"]\n        complete = select(rows, \"ChildCompleteReadBack\", label=role)\n        assert complete[\"payload\"][\"turn_id\"] == info[\"turn_id\"]\n        assert final[\"time\"] < stamp(stop) < complete[\"timestamp\"]\n        if role == \"supplement\":\n            assert parent_third[\"timestamp\"] < final[\"time\"]\n        child_file = assert answer[\"timestamp\"] == final[\"time\"]\n        assert answer[\"payload\"][\"phase\"] == \"final_answer\"\n        assert \"\".join(part.get(\"text\", \"\") for part in \"parentSessionId\": parent_id, \"parentRollout\": parent_file.relative_to(work / \"state\").as_posix(),\n            \"parentReplyReadBackTimes\": {label: stamp(entry) for label, entry in ## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_4e8fc9daf13b45daa37b85a422e3bb60`
~~~

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const work=load("timeline_work");
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`cat > ${work}/extract-code-mode.py <<'PY'
${String.raw`"""独立核对 Code Mode 的合成原文、身份、时间和落盘顺序。"""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from hashlib import sha256
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

ROOT = Path(sys.argv[1])
MESSAGES = (
    ("initial", "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_INITIAL\n\n子代理初次派发的合成原文。",
     "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_INITIAL_DONE\n\n初次回复原文。"),
    ("supplement", "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_SUPPLEMENT\n\n对同一子代理的合成补充。",
     "FOUNDRY_CODE_CHILD_SUPPLEMENT_DONE\n\n补充回复原文。"),
)
THIRD = "FOUNDRY_CODE_PARENT_CROSS_TURN"


def read_rows(path):
    return list(map(json.loads, path.read_text().splitlines()))


def one(rows):
    matches = list(rows)
    assert len(matches) == 1, matches
    return matches[0]


def select(rows, kind, **fields):
    return one(row for row in rows if row["kind"] == kind
               and all(row.get(name) == value for name, value in fields.items()))


def stamp(row):
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(row["wallNs"] / 1_000_000_000, UTC).isoformat(
        timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")


def match_hook(rows, event, **fields):
    return one(row for row in rows if row.get("configuredEvent") == event
               and all(row["payload"].get(name) == value for name, value in fields.items()))


def in_child_call(rows, event, text):
    return one(row for row in rows if row.get("configuredEvent") == event
               and isinstance(row["payload"].get("tool_input"), dict)
               and row["payload"]["tool_input"].get("message") == text)


def catalog_check(name, flag):
    rows = read_rows(ROOT / name / "observations.jsonl")
    tool = select(rows, "CodeDefinition")
    catalog = select(rows, "ToolCatalog")
    request = one(row for row in rows if row["kind"] == "Request"
                  and "code-discover" in row.get("output", {}))
    reply = request["output"]["code-discover"]
    assert tool["name"] == "exec" and tool["type"] == "custom"
    assert catalog["names"] == []
    assert any(part.get("text") == "FOUNDRY_CATALOG []" for part in reply)
    assert not any(row["kind"] == "RunCompleted" for row in rows)
    return {"dynamicToolsFlag": flag, "codeModeCatalogReply": reply,
            "requestedNestedOperations": ["spawn_agent", "followup_task", "send_input"],
            "nestedAgentsMatched": []}


def check(name, route):
    work = ROOT / name
    rows = read_rows(work / "observations.jsonl")
    installation = select(rows, "Install")
    done = select(rows, "RunCompleted")
    parent_id = done["sessionId"]
    parent_file = one((work / "state/sessions").rglob(f"*{parent_id}.jsonl"))
    parent = read_rows(parent_file)
    record = work / "repo" / done["record"]
    primary = record.read_bytes()
    assert primary.count(b"<!-- turn-") == 3
    if route == "nested":
        catalog = select(rows, "ToolCatalog")
        assert set(catalog["names"]) == {"multi_agent_v1__spawn_agent", "multi_agent_v1__send_input"}
        code_calls = [row["payload"]["call_id"] for row in parent if row["payload"].get("type") == "custom_tool_call"]
        assert code_calls == ["code-discover", "code-spawn", "code-followup"]
    else:
        tools = [row["name"] for row in rows if row["kind"] == "DirectTool"]
        assert tools == ["spawn_agent", "followup_task"]
        assert [row["payload"]["name"] for row in parent if row["payload"].get("type") == "function_call"] == tools
    reads = {part: select(rows, "ParentReadBack", turn=part) for part in ("first", "second", "third")}
    assert all(row["sessionId"] == parent_id for row in reads.values())
    assert sha256(primary).hexdigest() == reads["third"]["sha256"]
    third = match_hook(rows, "UserPromptSubmit", prompt=THIRD)
    parent_third = one(row for row in parent if row["payload"].get("type") == "task_started"
                       and row["payload"]["turn_id"] == third["payload"]["turn_id"])
    assert sha256(primary).hexdigest() != reads["second"]["sha256"]
    snapshots = ((select(rows, "Checkpoint", label="parent-first-done"),
                  select(rows, "Checkpoint", label="child-initial-done")),
                 (select(rows, "Checkpoint", label="parent-third-started"),
                  select(rows, "Checkpoint", label="child-supplement-cross-turn-done")))
    assert all(before["snapshot"] == after["snapshot"] for before, after in snapshots)

    events = []
    for (role, text, reply), parent_label in zip(MESSAGES, ("first", "second"), strict=True):
        pre = in_child_call(rows, "PreToolUse", text)
        post = in_child_call(rows, "PostToolUse", text)
        assert pre["payload"]["tool_use_id"] == post["payload"]["tool_use_id"]
        tool = post["payload"]
        called_id = tool["tool_use_id"]
        stop = match_hook(rows, "SubagentStop", last_assistant_message=reply)
        info = stop["payload"]
        assert pre["payload"]["turn_id"] == tool["turn_id"]
        assert tool["session_id"] == info["session_id"] == parent_id
        assert info["turn_id"] != tool["turn_id"]
        assert text.encode() not in primary and reply.encode() not in primary
        call = one((index, row) for index, row in enumerate(parent, 1)
                   if row["payload"].get("type") == "item_completed"
                   and row["payload"].get("item", {}).get("id") == called_id)
        assert call[1]["payload"]["turn_id"] == tool["turn_id"]
        activity = call[1]["payload"]["item"]
        if route == "nested":
            assert activity.get("type") == "CollabAgentToolCall"
            assert activity["prompt"] == text
            assert activity["receiver_thread_ids"] == [info["agent_id"]]
            response = json.loads(tool["tool_response"])
            if role == "initial":
                assert response["agent_id"] == info["agent_id"]
            else:
                assert response["submission_id"] == info["turn_id"]
            child_prompt = match_hook(rows, "UserPromptSubmit", agent_id=info["agent_id"], prompt=text)
            assert child_prompt["payload"]["turn_id"] == info["turn_id"]
        else:
            assert activity.get("type") == "SubAgentActivity"
            assert activity["agent_thread_id"] == info["agent_id"]

        sent = select(rows, "GateRelease", label=role)
        assert reads[parent_label]["wallNs"] < sent["wallNs"] < stop["wallNs"]
        if role == "supplement":
            assert reads["second"]["wallNs"] < third["wallNs"] < sent["wallNs"] < stop["wallNs"] < reads["third"]["wallNs"]
            assert parent_third["timestamp"] < stamp(sent)
        callback = stop["replyAtCallback"]
        assert callback["activeTurn"] == info["turn_id"]
        final = one(callback["matchingFinalAnswers"])
        assert final["sha256"] == sha256(reply.encode()).hexdigest()
        assert not callback["taskCompleteAlreadyReadable"]
        complete = select(rows, "ChildCompleteReadBack", label=role)
        assert complete["payload"]["turn_id"] == info["turn_id"]
        assert final["time"] < stamp(stop) < complete["timestamp"]
        if role == "supplement":
            assert parent_third["timestamp"] < final["time"]
        child_file = Path(info["agent_transcript_path"])
        with child_file.open("rb") as rollout:
            rollout.seek(final["byteOffset"])
            answer = json.loads(rollout.readline())
        assert answer["timestamp"] == final["time"]
        assert answer["payload"]["phase"] == "final_answer"
        assert "".join(part.get("text", "") for part in answer["payload"]["content"]) == reply
        events.append({"stage": role, "senderParentTurnId": tool["turn_id"], "childThreadId": info["agent_id"],
                       "childTurnId": info["turn_id"], "toolName": tool["tool_name"], "toolUseId": called_id,
                       "originalInputInBothToolHooks": text, "toolTarget": tool["tool_input"].get("target"),
                       "postToolResponse": tool["tool_response"],
                       "parentActivity": {"line": call[0], "type": activity["type"], "time": call[1]["timestamp"]},
                       "replyFromSubagentStop": reply, "subagentStopAt": stamp(stop), "replyAtCallback": callback,
                       "childTaskCompleteAt": complete["timestamp"],
                       "childRollout": child_file.relative_to(work / "state").as_posix()})
    assert events[0]["childThreadId"] == events[1]["childThreadId"]
    assert events[0]["childTurnId"] != events[1]["childTurnId"]
    return {"execution": route, "featureMode": installation["mode"], "version": installation["version"],
            "installedFileCount": len(installation["hashes"]),
            "eventModuleSha256": installation["hashes"]["recording/events.py"],
            "parentSessionId": parent_id, "parentRollout": parent_file.relative_to(work / "state").as_posix(),
            "parentReplyReadBackTimes": {label: stamp(entry) for label, entry in reads.items()},
            "thirdTurnStartedAt": parent_third["timestamp"], "thirdPromptHookAt": stamp(third),
            "recordStayedUnchangedBetweenParentActivityAtBothChildReplies": True, "events": events}


result = {
    "source": "cf8def3:packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording",
    "scope": "Linux Codex CLI 原生隔离交互，Code Mode 和补充回传，所有文本合成，时间为 UTC",
    "v1CodeMode": check("m1", "nested"),
    "v2CodeModeCatalogChecks": [catalog_check("cm2", False), catalog_check("md2", True)],
}
if len(sys.argv) > 2:
    result["v2Control"] = check("mc2", "separate-direct-tools")
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
`}
~~~

~~~text
  "command": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/subagent-recording-timeline.md\n@@\n-本轮确认了直接派发时的父会话输入来源，以及父会话空闲和活跃时的子代理回复回调。正式主记录的交错写入和分隔仍待接入并复验 SC-010、SC-011。后续补充消息和 Code Mode 中的派发未在本轮实测。 [验收标准](../spec.md#measurable-outcomes) · [早期子代理取证](content-source.md#子代理活动及父会话可见字段)\n+首轮探针确认了直接派发时的父会话输入来源，以及父会话空闲和活跃时的子代理回复回调。随后用下面的样例补验了 Code Mode 和补充消息。正式主记录的交错写入和分隔仍待接入并复验 SC-010、SC-011。 [验收标准](../spec.md#measurable-outcomes) · [早期子代理取证](content-source.md#子代理活动及父会话可见字段)\n+\n+## Code Mode 与补充消息补验\n+\n+2026-11-27 复用 Linux Codex CLI 0.172.0、维护源本地安装和回环合成样例。父代理在第一轮启动一个子代理，第二轮再向同一子代理发送不同的多行文本。父代理先保存第二轮回复，第三轮开始后才放行子代理的补充回复。原生回调的观测脚本在收到 `SubagentStop` 时立即读回合成子线程日志。 [结构化补验证据](acceptance/subagent-recording-code-mode.json)\n+\n+- **V1**。在 Code Mode 的 `exec` 中调用 `multi_agent_v1__spawn_agent` 和 `multi_agent_v1__send_input`，动态拼成含空行的消息。两次内部工具的父会话 `PreToolUse` 和 `PostToolUse` 都暴露逐字一致的 `tool_input.message`。父日志中的 `CollabAgentToolCall.id` 匹配工具回调的 `tool_use_id`，两条活动关联到同一个子线程。补充回包的 `submission_id` 对应补充回传的子线程轮次。\n+- **V2**。启用 Code Mode 后，两组 `exec` 工具目录查询（关闭及开启 `multi_agent_v2_dynamic_tools`）均没有匹配的派发和补充工具。同一 Code Mode 设置下，模型可从独立的 `collaboration` 工具入口使用 `spawn_agent` 和 `followup_task`。该入口实测取得两段原文，父日志以不同的 `SubAgentActivity` 调用 ID 将它们关联到同一子线程。两条链路的补充回复均跨到第三个父轮。\n+- **回复时间**。V1 和 V2 对照的初次与补充回复共四条。每次 `SubagentStop` 执行时，根据回调的 `agent_id` 和子线程 `turn_id`，从 `agent_transcript_path` 读到了唯一匹配回复全文的 `final_answer` 条目及其宿主时间。该条目早于回调接收时间，当轮 `task_complete` 均在回调之后落盘。配对以子线程和轮次为依据，回调带来的回复原文用于核对。\n+\n+以下是补充回复场景的时间，均为 UTC。\n+\n+| 事件 | V1 Code Mode | V2 独立协作工具 |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| 父第二轮回复已读回 | 01:58:58.442 | 02:00:30.200 |\n+| 父第三轮开始 | 01:58:58.722 | 02:00:30.367 |\n+| 子线程补充回复的宿主时间 | 01:58:59.435 | 02:00:30.926 |\n+| 收到补充回复的停止回调并读到宿主时间 | 01:58:59.608 | 02:00:31.072 |\n+| 子线程完成条目时间 | 01:58:59.630 | 02:00:31.094 |\n+\n+两次回传前后均比对了现行记录入口的文件清单、哈希和修改时间，分别一致。现有主记录继续保存三轮用户和主代理消息，子代理交错写入与分隔仍是正式接入的验收项。 [结构化补验证据](acceptance/subagent-recording-code-mode.json)\n*** End Patch"
}
~~~

~~~text
+
+## Code Mode 与补充消息补验
+
+2026-11-27 复用 Linux Codex CLI 0.172.0、维护源本地安装和回环合成样例。父代理在第一轮启动一个子代理，第二轮再向同一子代理发送不同的多行文本。父代理先保存第二轮回复，第三轮开始后才放行子代理的补充回复。原生回调的观测脚本在收到 \`SubagentStop\` 时立即读回合成子线程日志。 [结构化补验证据](acceptance/subagent-recording-code-mode.json)
+
+- **V1**。在 Code Mode 的 \`exec\` 中调用 \`multi_agent_v1__spawn_agent\` 和 \`multi_agent_v1__send_input\`，动态拼成含空行的消息。两次内部工具的父会话 \`PreToolUse\` 和 \`PostToolUse\` 都暴露逐字一致的 \`tool_input.message\`。父日志中的 \`CollabAgentToolCall.id\` 匹配工具回调的 \`tool_use_id\`，两条活动关联到同一个子线程。补充回包的 \`submission_id\` 对应补充回传的子线程轮次。
+- **V2**。启用 Code Mode 后，两组 \`exec\` 工具目录查询（关闭及开启 \`multi_agent_v2_dynamic_tools\`）均没有匹配的派发和补充工具。同一 Code Mode 设置下，模型可从独立的 \`collaboration\` 工具入口使用 \`spawn_agent\` 和 \`followup_task\`。该入口实测取得两段原文，父日志以不同的 \`SubAgentActivity\` 调用 ID 将它们关联到同一子线程。两条链路的补充回复均跨到第三个父轮。
+- **回复时间**。V1 和 V2 对照的初次与补充回复共四条。每次 \`SubagentStop\` 执行时，根据回调的 \`agent_id\` 和子线程 \`turn_id\`，从 \`agent_transcript_path\` 读到了唯一匹配回复全文的 \`final_answer\` 条目及其宿主时间。该条目早于回调接收时间，当轮 \`task_complete\` 均在回调之后落盘。配对以子线程和轮次为依据，回调带来的回复原文用于核对。
+
+以下是补充回复场景的时间，均为 UTC。
+
+| 事件 | V1 Code Mode | V2 独立协作工具 |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| 父第二轮回复已读回 | 01:58:58.442 | 02:00:30.200 |
+| 父第三轮开始 | 01:58:58.722 | 02:00:30.367 |

+| 子线程完成条目时间 | 01:58:59.630 | 02:00:31.094 |
+
+两次回传前后均比对了现行记录入口的文件清单、哈希和修改时间，分别一致。现有主记录继续保存三轮用户和主代理消息，子代理交错写入与分隔仍是正式接入的验收项。 [结构化补验证据](acceptance/subagent-recording-code-mode.json)
`.replace(/\\`/g,"`")+"*** End Patch";
~~~

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const repo="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init";
const checks=await Promise.allSettled([
  tools.exec_command({cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
from hashlib import sha256
import json, re, subprocess

base = Path("specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis")
path = base / "subagent-recording-timeline.md"
data_path = base / "acceptance/subagent-recording-code-mode.json"
document = path.read_text()
evidence = json.loads(data_path.read_text())
revision, source = evidence["source"].split(":", 1)
tracked = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{revision}:{source}/recording/events.py"])
assert tracked == (Path(source) / "recording/events.py").read_bytes()
for name in ("v1CodeMode", "v2Control"):
    run = evidence[name]
    assert run["eventModuleSha256"] == sha256(tracked).hexdigest()
    assert run["installedFileCount"] == 17
    event = run["events"][1]
    stamps = (run["parentReplyReadBackTimes"]["second"], run["thirdTurnStartedAt"],
              event["replyAtCallback"]["matchingFinalAnswers"][0]["time"], event["subagentStopAt"],
              event["childTaskCompleteAt"])
    assert list(stamps) == sorted(stamps)
    assert all(time[11:23] in document for time in stamps)
    assert all(not item["replyAtCallback"]["taskCompleteAlreadyReadable"] for item in run["events"])
    assert len({item["childThreadId"] for item in run["events"]}) == 1
for link in re.findall(r"\\]\\(([^)#]+)(?:#[^)]*)?\\)", document):
    assert (path.parent / link).is_file(), link
for file in (path, data_path):
    data = file.read_bytes()
    assert data.endswith(b"\\n") and all(line.rstrip() == line for line in data.splitlines())
print("补充回传时间、子线程关联、安装来源及证据链接均已读回核对")
PY`,workdir:repo,max_output_tokens:2600}),
~~~
